{"forms":[{"form":"cease-and-desist","tags":["canonical"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"She received a cease-and-desist from his solicitors soon after the podcast.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A written demand that a party stops and does not resume a certain behavior, such as copyright infringement, slander, or libel, threatening legal action if the recipient fails to do so."],"tags":["attributive"],"topics":["law"]},{"examples":[{"text":"His remarks did not stop the courts from issuing the cease-and-desist order.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An order by a government agency, sanctioned by a court, that one must stop and not resume a certain (usually illegal) behavior."],"tags":["US","attributive"],"topics":["law"]}],"upstream_line":343433,"upstream_offset":1665150301,"upstream_record_sha256":"4c9acd4c4e99d5bd6d135115a3e347f00180e8cafad94968740eb0ff2f3e43d4","word":"cease and desist"}
